【USTR Greer: new 301 tariffs unlikely to add economic impact】U.S. Trade Representative Greer said on Monday the newly implemented Section 301 tariffs are not expected to create economic effects beyond those already priced in. NZX 50 Retreats from Record High on Profit-Taking — New Zealand's stocks dropped 44 points, or 0.3%, to 13,806 in Tuesday morning deals, as traders took profit after the index hit a record high a day earlier, buoyed by easing oil prices. Traders remained cautious ahead of the Fed's monetary policy decision and China's PMI data, as well as the release of New Zealand's consumer confidence and business outlook data for July, due this week. However, easing oil prices due to moderating Middle East tensions helped limit the decline, as easing inflation concerns reduced expectations of further interest rate hikes. The broader index was mainly weighed down by losses in the financial, healthcare, industrial, and real estate sectors. Colonial Motor dropped 1.5%, Auckland International Airport fell 1.3%, Infratil decreased 1.2%, Ventia Services Group fell 0.8%, while Fisher & Paykel Healthcare and Henderson Far East Income each shed 0.6%.

【Apple overtakes NVIDIA to regain world's largest market cap】On Monday Apple (AAPL.O) closed above NVIDIA (NVDA.O) to become the world's most valuable company for the first time since April 2025. NVIDIA slid nearly 5%, cutting its market value to $4.77 tln as investor concerns over large AI infrastructure spending hit the AI-chip sector. Apple rose about 1% to a $4.95 tln market cap; it is scheduled to report quarterly results after the close on Thursday. NVIDIA had held the top spot since overtaking Microsoft in June 2025 and briefly reached $5 tln last October. Year-to-date through 2026, NVIDIA is up ~4% while Apple has gained ~24%, with investors favoring Apple's more cautious AI capex stance and preference for leasing compute over building owned infrastructure.

South Korea Consumer Sentiment Edges Higher — South Korea's Composite Consumer Sentiment Index edged up to 106.8 in July 2026 from 106.6 in June.. Households became slightly more optimistic about future income, with the corresponding index rising to 101 from 100, while expectations for future spending held steady at 110. Sentiment regarding current living standards slipped to 93 from 94, although expectations for future living conditions improved to 98 from 97. Consumers were slightly less optimistic about current domestic economic conditions, with the index falling to 84 from 86, while expectations for the economy over the next six months remained unchanged at 92. Inflation expectations were stable, with households projecting consumer prices to rise 2.7% over the next year, while three- and five-year inflation expectations both stood at 2.6%.

The bank retains a Buy rating but trims its PT to $480 from $510, citing the risk Microsoft may raise its capex outlook while merely maintaining — not upping — Azure growth guidance. UBS warned that higher spending coupled with limited revenue growth would be poorly received by markets. The note also flagged Microsoft’s exposure to OpenAI, substitution risk from open‑source models, and renewed scrutiny of its core productivity software business. US Stocks Mixed as Chipmakers Extend Selloff — US stock indices closed mixed on Monday as chipmakers extended their selloff ahead of this week's Fed decision and a busy slate of economic data and corporate earnings. The S&P 500 ended little changed, the Nasdaq 100 lost 0.3%, while the Dow gained 263 points. Nvidia fell 5% after announcing a $500 billion AI memory chip supply agreement with SK Hynix. Separately, the company guaranteed $250 billion for OpenAI to lease computing capacity from a major data center project, reviving concerns that circular AI financing deals could unravel if hyperscalers curb capital spending. Micron (-2.2%), AMD (-5.2%), Intel (-0.7%), and Sandisk (-11%) also declined. Meanwhile, easing concerns over tight financial conditions supported the broader market. The US and Iran paused strikes over the weekend, sending oil prices and Treasury yields lower, fading expectations of a Fed rate hike this week. Apple (+1.2%), Meta (-0.2%), Amazon (-0.3%), and Microsoft (+1.9%) traded mixed ahead of earnings releases.

### 2026-07-28 03:51:08  #MKT News #Market Regions #Japan & APAC

【Japan Securities Dealers Association to expand corporate bond trade disclosures】Japan Securities Dealers Association plans to broaden daily public disclosure of corporate bond trades, which it posts on its website showing prior-day contract price, trade amount and coupon. Current rules require disclosure for trades with face value ≥¥100m (≈$610,700) and ratings of AA or higher. A-rated bonds are disclosed only if all three conditions are met: issuance size ≥¥30bn, non-subordinated, and remaining maturity <20 years. Under the proposed changes the issuance threshold would be cut to ¥10bn and the remaining-maturity requirement removed. The association will seek public comment and could implement the changes as early as this year.

【Pimco擁抱曾經警告的AI熱潮 前提是鉅額融資條款由它主導】Dan Ivascyn希望參與為甲骨文規模達160億美元的大型資料中心園區提供融資的交易，但前提是交易必須按照他的方式設計。知情人士稱，去年年底，這位品浩(Pimco)的首席投資官願意讓公司成為這筆雄心勃勃融資交易的基石投資者，條件是將融資設計為收益率更具吸引力的長期債券，期限與甲骨文的租約相匹配。負責籌組交易的美國銀行最初曾就其他方案徵詢投資者意向，包括髮行期限更短、成本更低的債券，但時機頗為棘手。銀行此前已為甲骨文相關的資料中心專案提供了數十億美元貸款，而一些貸款機構一直難以將這些債務轉售出去。幾個月後，美國銀行重新回到談判桌，並願意按Pimco的條件安排一筆私募交易。

### 2026-07-28 02:33:34  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution #ORCL.N #MSFT.O #GOOG.O

【US firms: AI-driven discovery to roughly double software security vulnerabilities found in 2026 vs 2025】Software security vulnerabilities found in popular tech products in 2026 are expected to be about twice the number found in 2025, a surge market participants attribute mainly to advances in AI. The US National Vulnerability Database shows 45,207 vulnerabilities recorded from January through Monday, approaching 2025's full-year total after 2025 set a record. Oracle (ORCL.N) said its July fixes addressed 1,449 vulnerabilities versus 309 in the same month a year earlier; Microsoft (MSFT.O) disclosed 642 in July, nearly five times the year-earlier count; Google (GOOG.O) fixed 433 in a recent Chrome update versus 11 a year earlier. SentinelOne research scientist Gabriel Shapiro said AI tools are increasing the ability to find software bugs; Google Chrome engineering director Doug Turner said vulnerabilities are now being found at "unprecedented scale and speed" due to AI model progress and related investment.

Brent Continues to Fall — Brent extended losses and fell toward $88 per barrel on Monday, its lowest level in more than a week, as optimism grew that the conflict in the Middle East could soon ease. President Trump said the US was holding "good talks" with Iran and that there was a chance of reaching a deal, but warned that US strikes would resume if negotiations failed. The US paused its military strikes on Iran on Friday, following nearly two weeks of hostilities and Tehran said it had suspended its retaliatory military operations in response and entered talks with Oman regarding navigation through Hormuz. Supply conditions also improved after crude loadings resumed at the Caspian Pipeline Consortium terminal on Russia's Black Sea coast, a key export route for Kazakh oil that had recently been disrupted by Ukrainian drone attacks. 【Finnish industry official says U.S. tariffs while failing ILO obligations 'absurd'】Timo Wori, director for international trade and trade policy at the Federation of Finnish Industries, said the U.S. has not fully met its obligations under the International Labour Organization framework yet is imposing tariffs under the banner of "forced labor," calling the move absurd. He said the Trump administration sought legal cover for unilateral tariffs from a 1970s law to justify the "forced labor" claim. Wori warned U.S. tariff policy is hitting Finnish steel and pharmaceutical firms, damaging global trade ties and raising uncertainty for the global economy and business operations, and that the ultimate cost falls on U.S. consumers. He also expressed concern that tariffs are being used outside traditional trade policy—citing Greenland, Iran and NATO cooperation disputes—saying the "weaponization" of tariffs increases economic and security uncertainty worldwide.

【歐盟MARS機構：熱浪導致歐盟玉米、小麥產量預期下調】歐盟農業資源監測中心（MARS）表示，熱浪和乾旱天氣已導致西歐和中歐大部分地區的農作物產量前景惡化。高溫縮短了穀物灌漿期，導致冬季作物提前成熟；而夏季作物在關鍵生長期則遭遇了高溫和土壤水分不足的困擾。歐盟玉米產量預期較上次預測下調6%，目前低於五年平均水平2%。作為歐盟最大的玉米生產國，法國的玉米產量預期被下調13%，目前低於五年平均水平15%。歐盟小麥產量預期下調2%。法國小麥產量預期下調4%，目前比去年低8%。歐盟向日葵產量預期下調7%，但仍比去年高出4%。歐盟油菜籽產量預期下調1%。預計西歐將出現更多炎熱乾燥的天氣，這增加了農作物進一步減產的風險。

FTSE MIB Edges Up — The FTSE MIB rose 0.5% to 52,055 on Monday, tracking a broader improvement in risk appetite amid hopes for renewed diplomacy in the Middle East. Banking stocks gained on the session amid lower energy prices and easing concerns over inflationary pressures as sovereign bond yields retreated. UniCredit advanced 1.6%, following positive reviews from JPMorgan and Morgan Stanley. Amplifon gained 2.6% after JPMorgan raised its recommendation of the stock. Buzzi gained 2.8% on reconstruction activity prospects. In the luxury sector, Brunello Cucinelli (2.4%) and Campari (1.9%) climbed ahead of LVMH's earnings release. Ferrari outperformed with a 4.3% gain, leading gains on the index, following progress reports regarding its share buyback program. Meanwhile, technology and energy stocks underperformed, with Prysmian, Tenaris, and Eni all falling more than 2%, weighed by ongoing concerns over AI-related overspending and lower oil prices.

### 2026-07-27 23:53:11  #Trading Economics #Markets #Euro Area #Stock Market #Importance 1

European Stocks Inch Higher — European stocks closed slightly higher, outperforming North American counterparts with support from lower bond yields. The Euro STOXX 60 inched higher to 6,290 and the STOXX Europe 600 rose 0.2% to 645.6. Sovereign yields fell sharply as the momentary pause in strikes between Iran and the US reignited hopes of LNG and fuel exports from the region, limiting inflationary risks. Banks were sharply higher, with Santander and BNP Paribas adding over 2% while Intesa Sanapaolo rose 1% ahead of its earnings this week. Meanwhile, software producers jumped, tracking Wall Street counterparts ahead of AI hyperscaler earnings this week, with SAP soaring 7.8%. 【Capital Economics: Fed policy may diverge from ECB and BoE by 2027】Capital Economics economist Neil Shearing said in a note markets price just over 50bps of tightening for the Fed, the Bank of England and the European Central Bank by mid-2027, but policy paths could start to diverge as soon as next year. He said an end to the Iran conflict would likely push energy prices lower, leaving domestic fundamentals as the main driver of policy; underlying inflation pressures in the UK and euro area are weak, while the US faces a stronger inflation backdrop and fiscal policy remains relatively loose. Shearing added it is increasingly hard to justify market-implied tightening for the ECB and BoE, whereas the Fed may resume tightening sooner.

談及為何答應調解方的請求，特朗普表示：“這麼做不會有損失，也不存在風險。”他還提到，自己宣佈暫停軍事打擊後，國際油價應聲下跌，股市同步走高。

US Stocks Waver — US stock indices wavered on Monday amid a fresh selloff for chip producers ahead of a Fed decision and major economic updates this week. The S&P 500 swung to 0.3% lower while the Nasdaq 100 dropped 1%. Nvidia dropped 4.5% after announcing a $500 billion AI memory chip supply deal from SK Hynix. Separately, Nvidia guaranteed $250 billion for OpenAI to lease computing from a data center project, reigniting concerns that circular deals in AI computing could trigger contagion and unwind the AI trade should capex growth be halted. Micron, AMD, Indel, and Sandisk dropped between 6% and 10%. Still, the Dow added 0.5% on softer concerns of tight financial conditions. The US and Iran paused strikes over the weekend and fuel prices retreated from peaks. The consequent drop in yields reflected almost null expectations the Federal Reserve may deliver a rate hike this week, pushing back eventual tightening to September. Apple, Meta, Amazon, and Microsoft were higher ahead of earnings this week.

### 2026-07-27 22:50:41  #MKT News #Market Regions #Wall Street (US) #S&P500 #Impact mixed #Nasdaq100 #Dow

【CME lists single-stock futures for SpaceX and 54 U.S. names, raising access for shorts and leverage】CME today launched U.S. single-stock futures covering SpaceX and 54 other companies, offering 100-share standard contracts and 10-share micro contracts. Contracts are cash-settled, trade nearly round-the-clock and do not require borrowing stock, enabling long, short or hedged exposure without stock lend. For SpaceX this creates an additional pricing channel rather than changing fundamentals: futures will likely reflect pre-/post-market moves and news more quickly. SpaceX’s free float is limited and the share price has fallen from its peak; Ortex estimates roughly 56% of the free float is on loan and short interest is still rising. SpaceX is due to report its first post-IPO earnings on Aug. 4 and faces upcoming lock-up expiries. The launch’s market significance is primarily operational—easier entry for shorts, hedgers and leveraged funds—so spot–futures linkage and volatility around earnings and unlocks could be amplified in the near term.

### 2026-07-27 22:49:34  #市場 #央行 #國際

【美銀：成功捍衛日元需日本央行釋放鷹派訊號】美國銀行證券日本外匯及利率首席策略師山田周介（Shusuke Yamada）表示，要成功捍衛日元匯率，僅靠財務省的匯市干預遠遠不夠，日本央行必須釋放明確的鷹派政策訊號。他指出，隨著美元兌日元匯率逼近165關口，日本當局干預匯市的風險正顯著上升。山田周介在最新研報中分析稱，儘管7月份加息的機率極低，但日本央行決策層需透過前瞻指引，有效提振市場對9月份加息的預期。目前，利率互換市場定價顯示，9月份加息25個基點的機率僅為27%。該策略師強調，165是美元兌日元匯率下一個潛在的干預門檻。若放任匯率大幅突破該水平，將被市場解讀為日本當局缺乏捍衛日元的決心，進而可能引發加速拋售日元的連鎖反應。目前，美元兌日元匯率在163.70附近交投，已逼近上世紀80年代中期以來的歷史低位。此外，大規模匯市干預還可能引發市場對日本國債遭拋售的擔憂。山田周介指出，從貨幣外交的角度來看，任何實質性的外匯干預實際上都會增加日本央行進一步收緊貨幣政策的壓力。在政治層面，隨著日本首相高市早苗的支援率出現下滑，允許日元進一步貶值的政治代價正在攀升。山田周介總結認為，日本當局正接近一個必須實質性加強日元捍衛力度的臨界點。

### 2026-07-27 22:47:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Dallas Fed Manufacturing Index #Importance 1

US Dallas Fed Manufacturing Activity Rises in July — The Dallas Fed's general business activity index for Texas manufacturing rose to 1.3 in July 2026, up from 0 in June. Broader conditions strengthened, with the company outlook index jumping 11 points to 13.4 as uncertainty eased (6.4 vs. 10.9). Manufacturing activity accelerated, with the production index rising to 10.1 from 4.1, supported by stronger new orders (6.4 vs. 2.3) and shipments (8.8 vs. 7.1). Meanwhile, finished goods inventories declined (1.5 vs. 7.8), while delivery times lengthened slightly (2.3 vs. 0.9). Labor market conditions remained broadly stable, although employment (12.2 vs. 13.9) and hours worked (4.3 vs. 5.9) eased, while the wages and benefits index climbed (30.8 vs 25.8). Price pressures remained elevated despite slight moderation in prices paid and received. Looking ahead, firms continue to expect strong manufacturing activity over the next six months, with future production (34.6) and general business activity (26.5) remaining little changed.

【伊朗議員：阿曼無權單方面決定霍爾木茲海峽通航】當地時間7月27日，伊朗議會國家安全和外交政策委員會委員胡茲裡安（Khazarian）發表宣告，從國際法與歷史條約角度強調，阿曼在未經伊朗協調的情況下，無權單方面定義和執行霍爾木茲海峽的船舶通行規則。胡茲裡安指出，儘管伊朗與阿曼在1974年已根據等分線原則劃定了海底大陸架邊界，但兩國寬度不足24海里的領海水域至今尚未完成劃界。這意味著，若該水域達到24海里寬度，將分別構成伊朗12海里領海與阿曼12海里領海，但在當前未劃界狀態下，任何單方面行動均缺乏法理基礎。他進一步援引歷史檔案說明，兩國於1974年3月7日在阿曼蘇丹卡布斯訪德黑蘭期間發表聯合宣告，明確以“安全合作”取代“海域劃界”，在霍爾木茲海峽實行共同管轄。這一安排實質上確立了兩國在該海峽的“共同使用權”，任何涉及海峽通航的重大決定均需雙方協商一致。

### 2026-07-27 22:29:03  #公司 #國際

【亞馬遜申請部署5000顆衛星網路 進軍手機直連市場加劇與SpaceX競爭】亞馬遜公司已正式向美國聯邦通訊委員會（FCC）提交申請，計劃發射一個由5000顆衛星組成的“直連裝置”網路，旨在從太空直接向智慧手機等移動終端提供通訊服務，從而進一步加劇其與SpaceX在衛星網際網路領域的競爭。根據亞馬遜提交的申請檔案，若獲得監管機構批准，該低地球軌道網路將實現全球覆蓋，能夠在傳統地面訊號塔無法觸及的偏遠地區，為智慧手機及其他移動裝置提供訊息傳遞、資料傳輸及緊急救援等關鍵服務。此舉被視為亞馬遜在幾個月前公佈擬以116億美元收購衛星公司Globalstar Inc.後的又一重要戰略動作。該收購交易一旦完成，將使亞馬遜獲得能夠直接向手機傳輸資料的關鍵頻譜使用權。在商業合作方面，亞馬遜表示計劃繼續尋求與傳統電信運營商的結盟。目前，亞馬遜已與沃達豐集團和DirecTV等公司建立合作伙伴關係，允許其向客戶提供基於衛星的服務。此外，亞馬遜旗下的Amazon Leo網路已與蘋果公司達成協議，將為iPhone和Apple Watch提供衛星通訊服務。這一佈局直接對標SpaceX的星鏈（Starlink）計劃。SpaceX此前已與T-Mobile US Inc.達成合作，而同樣致力於構建直連裝置網路的AST SpaceMobile公司也已與威瑞森通訊（Verizon）和美國電話電報公司（AT&T）簽署了相關協議。

### 2026-07-27 22:27:47  #公司 #國際

【美國6月核心資本品訂單超預期增長 AI投資與國防支出支撐上半年企業投資】美國商務部7月27日公佈的資料顯示，美國6月核心資本品訂單環比增長0.9%，超出市場預期，為2026年上半年的企業資本投資畫上穩健句號。5月資料經上修後增幅達1.9%。這一剔除飛機和軍用裝置後的核心指標，反映出美國私營部門裝置投資意願持續強勁。今年以來，受人工智慧（AI）相關支出激增及地緣衝突推動的國防訂單增長雙重驅動，美國企業投資表現持續堅挺。資料顯示，計算機、通訊裝置、電氣裝置以及初級金屬製品訂單均錄得增長。其中，AI基礎設施建設成為拉動企業裝置需求的核心動力。今年4月，Alphabet、Meta、微軟和亞馬遜四大科技巨頭相繼宣佈，計劃在2026年合計投入高達7250億美元用於AI相關資本支出。與此同時，地緣政治緊張局勢也推升了美國國防相關訂單。不過，從更寬口徑來看，涵蓋商用飛機和軍用裝備在內的全部耐用品訂單6月僅環比增長0.3%，不及市場預期，顯示出航空及國防以外部分領域的需求相對溫和。

【美銀：美股二季度財報季開局強勁 盈利超預期率創2021年來新高】美國銀行證券股票及量化策略師薩維塔·蘇布拉馬尼安（Savita Subramanian）在7月27日釋出的最新備忘錄中指出，隨著第二季度財報季火熱開局，標普500指數成分股公司的業績表現超出預期，整體盈利增速創下多年來的新高。資料顯示，在目前已公佈業績的標普500指數成分股中，高達80%的企業每股收益（EPS）超出市場預期。蘇布拉馬尼安表示，這是自2021年以來同期業績超預期比例最高的一次。目前，標普500指數成分股的每股收益同比增長率已達24%，較7月1日的統計水平進一步提升了2個百分點。隨著更多企業陸續披露財報，預計該指數整體的每股收益同比增長率將進一步攀升至28%。在企業盈利指引方面，市場同樣釋放出積極訊號。7月份以來，企業釋出的每股收益指引高於市場普遍預期的數量，是低於預期數量的1.3倍，這一比例顯著高於7月份的歷史平均水平。蘇布拉馬尼安特別強調，未來三個月的業績指引比率正朝著1.9倍邁進，若這一趨勢得以確認，將創下自2021年以來的最高水平，進一步印證了當前美股企業盈利的強勁韌性。

### 2026-07-27 22:05:11  #MKT News

【MIIT issues "Small, Fast, Light, Precise" digital products and services cultivation guide (2026)】The Ministry of Industry and Information Technology (MIIT) issued the 2026 edition of its "small, fast, light, precise" digital products and services cultivation guide. The guide directs service providers to align offerings with Chinese SMEs’ digital-transformation needs—innovation, quality improvement, cost reduction, efficiency gains, green transition and security—and to improve supply–demand matching. It will serve as a reference for local SME authorities when selecting and nurturing such products and services. The term "small, fast, light, precise" is defined as miniaturized, rapid, lightweight and precise. The guide requires a demand-driven approach focused on SMEs’ transformation pain points; encourages local tailoring of cultivation standards and a classified/graded support system; calls for stronger policy coordination and resource pooling; and urges acceleration of a multi-party, complementary SME digital transformation service ecosystem.

TSX Hits Record High as Oil Prices Tumble — The S&P/TSX Composite Index gained 0.5% to trade above 35,500 on Monday, reaching a record high as oil prices tumbled following a pause in hostilities between the US and Iran. Crude prices fell after both countries halted strikes over the weekend amid renewed diplomatic efforts, with President Donald Trump reportedly open to restarting peace talks. The decline in oil prices eased energy-driven inflation concerns, pushing Canadian bond yields lower and supporting financials. RBC, BMO, and CIBC gained about 1%, while TD Bank and Scotiabank added more than 0.5%. Gold prices advanced as lower US Treasury yields boosted demand for the metal, lifting mining stocks. Agnico Eagle, Barrick, and WPM each gained about 1%. Shopify climbed more than 5%, tracking gains in US software stocks. In contrast, energy shares declined on lower oil prices, with Suncor and Cenovus losing nearly 3%. Celestica (+0.3%) and Gibson Energy (-1.2%) are scheduled to report earnings after the market close.

### 2026-07-27 22:03:33  #觀點 #國際

【摩根大通：超大規模資料中心債券供應激增致投資者持觀望態度】摩根大通最新研報指出，儘管超大規模資料中心運營商的債券利差與收益率持續走闊，但受市場對債券發行量持續激增的預期影響，投資者目前對二級市場此類債券持謹慎觀望態度。摩根大通策略師納撒尼爾·羅森鮑姆在週一釋出的報告中披露，上週美國流動性指數顯示，超大規模債券利差大幅擴大16個基點至151個基點，而該指數其他成分股利差僅微擴1個基點至91個基點，超大規模債券收益率相對提升61%。目前，投資級超大規模資料中心運營商的債券收益率已較BB級高收益債券（垃圾級最高評級）高出80個基點。報告分析認為，當前的估值水平理論上應開始吸引跨界投資者入場，但市場普遍存在“等待更優定價”的博弈心態。投資者普遍預期後續主要發行定價可能更低，因此傾向於推遲買入。同時，市場原本希望利差的擴大能促使發行方放緩發債節奏或使發行更具規律性，但發行人往往將利差擴大視為整體資本支出與融資成本中佔比較小的部分，發債意願並未受到實質性抑制。

【美國解除對墨西哥活牛進口禁令 泰森食品與JBS股價大幅上漲】隨著市場預期美國將恢復從墨西哥進口活牛，美國主要肉類加工企業股價週一集體走高。此前，為防範致命寄生蟲新大陸螺旋蠅的傳播，美國對墨西哥活牛實施了長達一年多的進口禁令。美國農業部上週五宣佈，將從8月24日起分階段重新開放美墨邊境的活牛進口口岸。這一政策調整旨在緩解美國國內因持續乾旱和進口禁令雙重擠壓而導致的牛源短缺問題。受此訊息提振，市場迅速消化了供應恢復的預期，前期因供應緊缺而積聚的多頭倉位遭到集中平倉，活牛期貨價格應聲下跌。供應端的寬鬆預期直接利好下游肉類加工商。在過去一年中，由於美國本土牛群規模降至75年來最低水平，加之墨西哥活牛進口渠道被切斷，肉類加工企業為爭奪稀缺的屠宰原料不得不支付高昂溢價，利潤空間受到嚴重擠壓。截至美東時間週一上午，肉類加工板塊全線飄紅。其中，JBS股價漲幅居前，達到6.8%；泰森食品股價大漲5.8%；荷美爾食品公司上漲1.9%；朝聖者的驕傲上漲1.5%；史密斯菲爾德食品公司上漲1%。

### 2026-07-27 21:51:17  #公司

【淘金互動易腦機介面技術加速落地，這家公司正與國內下游腦機介面客戶開展技術合作】腦機介面技術加速落地，相關概念股表現強勢，創新醫療(002173)走出三天兩板。訊息面上，日前，我國科研團隊釋出一款新型腦電訊號採集裝置，在全球範圍內首次實現跨地域上千人同步腦電訊號採集，使得神經大模型訓練與腦機介面通用技術研發邁出關鍵一步。從上市公司來看，近日，多家企業在互動平臺回覆腦機介面布局情況。其中，美好醫療(301363)表示，公司正與國內下游腦機介面客戶深入開展工藝探索與技術合作，全力協助其從研發驗證走向規模化商業落地。人工耳蝸被視為腦機介面技術在臨床神經調控領域的重要應用方向之一。公司作為全球人工耳蝸龍頭企業的核心戰略供應商，擁有近15年的深度協作經驗。雙方在產品研發設計及量產轉化等方面建立了長期深入的合作，隨著客戶業務規模穩步增長，公司相關業務也將保持穩定發展。 另外，偉思醫療(688580)表示，公司在腦機介面領域聚焦“非侵入式”技術路線，致力於構建“診斷—治療—康復”的全週期閉環體系。公司將腦電（EEG）採集解碼技術與經顱磁刺激、康復機器人及功能性電刺激等裝置深度融合，以中風康復為例，患者僅需透過“運動想象”即可觸發康復機器人，形成“意念—執行—強化”的精準閉環，這正是未來神經康復的重要演進方向。而海格通訊(002465)表示，公司已經發布首款自研消費級產品——智慧安睡儀，為消費者構建可感知、可分析、可反饋、可持續最佳化的全週期主動睡眠管理新體驗，目前正處於市場推廣階段。

### 2026-07-27 21:46:48  #其他

【工業和資訊化部發文提升中小企業數字化轉型服務供給】工業和資訊化部27日對外公佈《“小快輕準”數字化產品和服務培育指引（2026年版）》，旨在引導服務商緊密圍繞中小企業數字化轉型需求，促進數字化產品與服務供需適配，加快中小企業數字化轉型。 “小快輕準”，即小型化、快速化、輕量化、精準化。指引要求，堅持需求牽引，以中小企業數字化轉型需求為導向，緊扣轉型痛點；鼓勵地方因地制宜細化培育標準，建立分類分級培育體系；加強政策協同和資源統籌，加快構建多方參與、優勢互補的中小企業數字化轉型服務供給生態。 指引圍繞小型化、快速化、輕量化、精準化以及數智化、共性基礎要求等部署一系列舉措，包括針對細分行業構建工業機理模型或行業知識庫；產品操作介面應簡單易用，充分考慮中小企業認知習慣；鼓勵產品深度融合人工智慧技術；杜絕隱性收費與捆綁銷售等。 組織實施方面，指引提出，要依託全國中小企業數字化轉型服務平臺及地方服務平臺、促進中心等載體，建設“小快輕準”數字化產品優質資源池，促進優質產品跨區域流通共享，降低中小企業選型成本和試錯風險等。（新華社）

【住房城鄉建設部：進一步加強城市橋樑安全管理工作】住房城鄉建設部辦公廳釋出關於進一步加強城市橋樑安全管理工作的通知。通知提出，加強汛期橋樑安全管理。當前，全國已全面進入汛期，城市橋樑主管部門要加強與氣象、水利、自然資源等部門協同聯動，紮實做好極端天氣以及涉水、涉地質災害等風險提示及防範應對工作。重點聚焦橋樑基礎沖刷、邊坡穩定和暢通排水設施等，全面開展防汛安全風險隱患排查。主動協調河道管理部門，及時清理河道內堆積物及可能影響橋樑安全的大型漂流物，確保行洪暢通。強降雨期間，加密巡檢頻次，發現問題及時處置。降雨結束後，及時排查橋樑受損情況，儘快修復受損設施，總結防汛經驗，完善應急預案。強化橋樑定期檢測和加固改造。城市橋樑主管部門要指導督促管養單位加強城市橋樑養護工作。嚴格落實定期檢測要求，並加強過程管理，確保檢測資料真實可靠、技術狀況評估定級合理，橋樑檢測評估報告應完整歸檔備查。對存在安全隱患的，及時採取有效措施妥善進行處置。Ⅰ類養護橋樑被評定為不合格的、Ⅱ-Ⅴ類養護橋樑綜合評定或上、下部結構評定為D、E級的，要加快實施維修加固或改擴建，消除安全隱患。

【AI需求驅動 香港6月商品出口貨值同比增53.4%】香港特區政府統計處27日釋出的對外商品貿易統計數字顯示，2026年6月香港整體出口和進口貨值同比分別增長53.4%和45.4%。 統計顯示，6月香港商品整體出口貨值為6411億港元，進口貨值為6930億港元，有形貿易逆差為520億港元，相當於商品進口貨值的7.5%。 前6個月，香港商品整體出口和進口貨值同比分別上升39.1%和40.6%，有形貿易逆差2946億港元，相當於商品進口貨值的7.9%。 按國家地區分析，6月香港輸往亞洲的整體出口貨值同比增長54.4%，其中對新加坡同比上升83%、對臺灣同比上升79.9%、對內地同比上升59.2%，輸往美國的整體出口貨值也同比上升114.3%；來自大部分主要供應地的進口貨值相比去年同期錄得升幅，其中韓國同比增長176.7%、越南同比增長106.8%、印度同比增長95.4%。 香港貿易發展局研究總監龐溟表示，上半年香港出口維持強勁增長，主要受惠於全球人工智慧加速應用，帶動電子產品需求持續強勁。與此同時，6月香港輸往亞洲以及內地、美國等主要出口目的地的整體出口貨值同比增速相比5月均有所加快。（新華社）

Swiss Bond Yield Falls on Oil Decline — Switzerland's 10-year government bond yield fell toward 0.4%, tracking declines in global bond yields and oil prices after the US and Iran paused hostilities, raising hopes for a diplomatic resolution that could de-escalate the conflict and restore shipping through the Strait of Hormuz. Meanwhile, the Trump administration imposed new tariffs on Swiss imports while keeping them within its previously announced 12.5% ceiling. On the monetary policy front, the Swiss National Bank is expected to keep its policy rate at 0% through 2027, with negative rates remaining a contingency rather than the base case. The SNB left rates unchanged in June, and while most economists do not expect the first rate hike until early 2028, currency markets are pricing in an increase by March next year. Swiss inflation slowed to 0.5% in June and is forecast to peak at just 0.8%, remaining comfortably within the SNB's 0%-2% target range.

Swiss Franc Stays Near One-Year Low — The Swiss franc hovered around $0.82, remaining close to its weakest level in more than a year against the dollar, as investors weighed easing tensions between the US and Iran and the sharp decline in oil prices, which boosted risk appetite and reduced demand for safe-haven assets. Meanwhile, the Trump administration imposed new tariffs on Swiss imports while keeping them within its previously announced 12.5% ceiling. ### 2026-07-27 21:29:03  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Currency #Importance 1

Dollar Pares Most Losses — The dollar index pared early losses to trade little changed around 101.4 on Monday, hovering near a one-month high as developments in the Middle East continued to dominate market sentiment. The US suspended its nearly two-week campaign of strikes against Iran late on Friday. In response, Tehran halted its retaliatory military operations and held talks with Oman on ensuring the safe passage of shipping through the Strait of Hormuz. These developments triggered a decline in oil prices, easing concerns over further supply disruptions and a renewed inflationary spiral. As a result, investors scaled back expectations for Fed policy tightening. The central bank is widely expected to leave the federal funds rate unchanged, although markets currently assign around a 33% probability to a rate hike. Looking ahead, a September increase remains the base case, with traders pricing in nearly an 80% chance of such a move.

### 2026-07-27 21:24:49  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Tumbles as US and Iran Halt Strikes — Brent crude fell sharply to as low as $87.6 per barrel on Monday before trimming some losses to trade around $90.8, as signs of easing geopolitical tensions reduced concerns over supply disruptions. The US paused its attacks against Iran, while Tehran said it had halted its retaliatory military strikes in response and engaged in talks with Oman regarding the Strait of Hormuz. Supply conditions also improved after crude loadings resumed at the Caspian Pipeline Consortium terminal on Russia's Black Sea coast, a key export hub for Kazakh oil that had recently been disrupted by Ukrainian drone attacks. Despite Monday's decline, Brent remains up nearly 25% this month as supply disruptions expanded from the Strait of Hormuz to the Red Sea, an increasingly important alternative route for Saudi Arabian crude exports. Yemen's Iran-backed Houthi militants claimed to have targeted Saudi Aramco-linked facilities in Jizan and Yanbu, although neither Saudi Arabia nor Aramco confirmed the attacks.

### 2026-07-27 21:21:31  #MKT News #Macro & Rates

【Sources: Swiss National Bank plans to keep policy rate at zero through end-2027】Sources said the Swiss National Bank plans to hold its key policy rate at zero through end-2027, only considering hikes thereafter based on current inflation projections and assuming no major new shocks. Recent CHF/EUR weakness and the Swiss–eurozone rate differential factored into the view. Sources said negative rates remain a contingency if the outlook deteriorates, but are not the baseline. They added zero rates have not materially hurt Swiss banks’ profitability. The SNB left rates unchanged at its June meeting and had signalled it expected rates to remain unchanged through the end of next year.

Treasury Yields Retreat — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note fell to 4.65% on Monday after reaching its highest level since January 2025 last week, helped by declining oil prices. The US suspended its nearly two-week campaign of strikes against Iran late on Friday, although no official announcement was made. As a result, Tehran halted its retaliatory military operations and held talks with Oman on ensuring shipping through the Strait of Hormuz. These developments alleviated concerns over further supply disruptions and a renewed inflationary spiral, prompting investors to scale back expectations for Fed's tightening. Investors are also awaiting the Federal Reserve's monetary policy decision later this week. Looking ahead, a September increase remains the base case, with traders pricing in nearly an 80% chance of such a move.

【摩根士丹利：星艦第13次試飛提振市場信心 維持SpaceX增持評級及300美元目標價】摩根士丹利在最新研報中指出，儘管SpaceX星艦第13次試飛中助推器未能實現完美回收，但此次任務仍顯著提振了市場信心。該行強調，試飛成功完成了新一代星鏈V3衛星部署、在軌發動機重啟以及迄今最平穩的海上濺落等關鍵驗證目標，標誌著星艦系統向完全可複用邁出了重要一步。摩根士丹利認為，若星艦第14次試飛能實現發射塔對飛船上級的直接捕獲，將成為推動SpaceX估值上行的下一個關鍵催化劑。基於星艦技術驗證的持續進展及其在AI基礎設施、全球衛星連線等領域的長期潛力，該行維持對SpaceX股票的“增持”評級，並重申300美元的目標價。研報同時指出，星艦的快速迭代與低成本發射能力是SpaceX構建“太空+AI”複合生態的核心基石。隨著星鏈V3衛星組網加速及軌道算力佈局推進，SpaceX有望進一步鞏固其在商業航天與人工智慧基礎設施領域的雙重領先地位。

Silver Rallies as Falling Oil Prices Ease Rate Hike Concerns — Silver climbed more than 1% toward $59 an ounce on Monday as easing tensions between the US and Iran triggered a sharp decline in oil prices, helping to ease inflation concerns and temper expectations of further aggressive Federal Reserve tightening ahead of this week's policy meeting. Brent crude retreated from two-month highs after Washington and Tehran paused hostilities, fueling hopes for a diplomatic resolution that could de-escalate the conflict and restore shipping through the Strait of Hormuz. Iran also said it would refrain from further attacks as long as the US maintained its pause in military operations. Investors are now focused on the Federal Reserve's policy decision on Wednesday. Markets currently price in around a 30% chance of a rate hike this week and roughly an 80% probability of another increase in September.

【中國風電正搶裝全球】半年攬單4.4GW，在手訂單超百億，版圖跨越三大洲。這不是光伏，也不是鋰電池，而是風電交出的“出海成績單”。江蘇射陽，一批大型海上智慧風機正加緊組裝除錯。它們的目的地是越南永隆128兆瓦近海風電專案——建成後將是越南及東南亞單機容量領先的近海風電專案群。遠景能源海上產品總經理鄧恆介紹，這是越南首次採用大葉輪直徑、高單機容量海上智慧風機。2026年1-6月，遠景能源新增海外訂單超4.4GW，覆蓋北非、南歐、東南亞等全球多個區域。唐山曹妃甸，大金重工投建的深遠海海工基地今年順利投產。這是全球首個超大型海工產品全室內建造工廠，可批次製造匹配15MW—25MW大功率機組的深遠海固定式、浮式風電基礎，著力解決深遠海風電開發中基礎裝備成本高、交付慢、產業鏈協同難等痛點。大金重工副總裁姜海濤透露，截至目前，大金重工海外在手訂單規模超100億元，產品遠銷30多個國家和地區。記者走訪多家企業瞭解到，今年以來海外訂單顯著增長，出海佈局持續提速，海外市場已成為行業增長的重要引擎。（央視財經）

【韓國執政黨黨首選舉進入倒計時“三強”競爭】韓國執政黨共同民主黨定於8月17日舉行全國代表大會，選舉新任黨首。隨著選舉進入倒計時，三名候選人前國務總理金民錫、前黨首鄭清來和宋永吉27日前往地方拉票，選戰愈發激烈。 新任黨首將掌握2028年韓國國會議員選舉該黨候選人提名權。據韓國媒體27日報道，上述三名候選人當天分別前往忠清北道和忠清南道等地，與該黨成員舉行座談會等活動，宣傳競選主張。忠清北道和忠清南道被視為這次黨首選舉的“激戰區”，三人均希望在當地佔得先機。 按韓國媒體說法，隨著黨首選舉日期臨近，候選人之間加緊相互攻訐。金民錫27日稱，鄭清來藉助黨外勢力干涉本黨選舉。鄭清來對此予以反駁，稱自己將“守護共同民主黨”。宋永吉則稱，如果鄭清來再次當選黨首，將加劇共同民主黨與政府間的矛盾。 近期，共同民主黨曝出“內訌”傳聞。今年6月地方選舉後，該黨多名國會議員以共同民主黨候選人未能贏得首爾市長等關鍵職位為由，要求鄭清來辭去黨首職務。另有人指責，鄭清來將重點放在爭取強硬派支持者上，未能全力支援總統李在明施政。面對壓力，鄭清來仍決定尋求連任。鄭清來於6月辭去黨首職務，以便參選。根據共同民主黨相關規定，黨首競選連任前必須辭職。（新華社）

### 2026-07-27 20:37:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Mexico #Balance of Trade #Importance 2

Mexico Trade Surplus Widens Above Forecasts — Mexico's trade surplus widened to $4.09 billion in June 2026 from $0.51 billion a year earlier, well above market expectations of $2.28 billion. Exports surged 34.4% year-on-year to $72.5 billion, driven by a 34.1% increase in non-oil shipments and a 43.4% rise in oil exports. Manufactured exports climbed 35.3%, led by metal products (40.9%), electrical and electronic equipment (19.7%), food, beverages and tobacco (14.5%), and automotive products (7.6%). In contrast, agricultural and fisheries exports fell 2.8%. Imports rose 28% to $68.46 billion, reflecting strong domestic demand. Intermediate goods imports increased 30.9%, consumer goods imports advanced 23.5%, boosted by a 66% surge in petroleum products, while capital goods imports rose 8.8%.

### 2026-07-27 20:37:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Durable Goods Orders #Importance 3

US Durable Goods Orders Rise Less Than Expected But Core Beat — New orders for US-manufactured durable goods increased 0.3% month-over-month to $334.77 billion in June 2026, rebounding from a revised 4% slump in May but much lower than forecasts of a 1.6% jump. Orders for capital goods went up 1.1% and increases were also seen in orders for primary metals (1.1%), computers and electronics (3.1%), and electrical equipment, appliances, and components (0.9%). On the other hand, orders declined for transportation (-0.2%), mostly motor vehicle and parts (-0.6%), fabricated metal products (-0.5%), and machinery (-0.1%). Excluding transportation, durable goods orders were up 0.6%. Meanwhile, orders for non-defense capital goods excluding aircraft, a closely watched proxy for business spending plans, rose by 0.9%, following an upwardly revised 1.9% rise in May and above forecasts of 0.8%. Business investment has remained resilient this year, supported by robust spending on AI and a war-related increase in US defence orders.

### 2026-07-27 20:22:21  #國際

【新研究說太陽中銀元素丰度比此前估計更高】 斯德哥爾摩訊息：瑞典烏普薩拉大學日前宣佈，該校研究人員和國際同行發現，太陽中的銀元素丰度比此前估計高出55%。 太陽主要由氫和氦兩種元素構成，碳、鐵、銀等元素佔太陽總質量的比例雖然很低，卻能為研究太陽和其他天體演化提供重要線索。 為確定太陽中的銀元素丰度，研究人員利用光譜學方法，分析太陽光穿過太陽大氣後光譜中相應元素的特徵譜線。以往的估算主要基於較為簡化的太陽大氣模型，此次研究則更深入分析了太陽大氣動力學模型，並更細緻考慮銀原子與光及其他粒子之間的相互作用。新的測算結果顯示，太陽中的銀元素丰度比此前估計的高55%。 研究人員說，過去科學界測得的太陽中銀元素丰度明顯低於一些隕石中的丰度，太陽與這些隕石均源於約46億年前的相同氣體和塵埃雲，銀元素丰度在理論上應較為接近。新的估值更接近隕石資料，有助於解釋長期存在的“銀元素缺失”問題。 研究人員表示，下一步將把相關方法應用於其他恆星，以進一步瞭解銀元素在宇宙中的形成、分佈和變化。相關論文已發表於歐洲學術期刊《天文和天體物理學》。(新華社)

【國內首個抗失眠創新藥法贊雷生在美團買藥線上首發】7月27日，抗失眠創新藥法贊雷生片（商品名：百樂眠 ）在美團買藥開啟線上首發。這是國內首個自主研發的雙重食慾素受體拮抗劑（DORA），可用於治療以入睡困難或睡眠維持困難為特徵的成人失眠患者。傳統安眠藥多透過廣泛抑制中樞神經系統起效，常帶來次日嗜睡、依賴性和戒斷反應等問題。法贊雷生片作為新一代DORA類藥物，透過雙重阻斷食慾素受體，精準抑制過度覺醒訊號，不破壞自然睡眠結構，幫助患者恢復接近生理狀態的睡眠。美團買藥資料顯示，自新一代 DORA 類促眠藥上線以來，平臺相關訂單量複合增長率保持在兩位數，使用者對創新睡眠治療方案的需求正在快速釋放。

【中國信通院葛雨明：自動駕駛研發對極端場景工況等高質量資料存在更大訴求】“我們也注意到當前自動駕駛研發資料已不單純以量取勝，而是對極端場景工況等高質量資料存在更大訴求。”在7月27日2026世界智慧網聯汽車大會媒體圓桌會上，中國資訊通訊研究院車聯網與智慧交通研究部主任葛雨明回答記者等媒體的問題時說。從企業層面看。一是加速供給模式向多元協同演進。在依託資料採集車或量產資料回傳的實車採集模式基礎上，進一步發揮路側感知、通訊基礎設施全天候、多視角的部署優勢，探索將更多路側感知能夠獲取的真實複雜交通場景資料用於自動駕駛研發。二是加強生成式自動駕駛資料生產技術研發與應用。加速探索世界模型等資料生成技術研發，支援惡劣天氣、特殊路段、複雜交通博弈、危險行為等長尾場景的資料泛化合成。三是探索可信可控的資料跨主體流通。透過汽車行業可信資料空間等資料流通平臺設施，探索價值共享、安全合規的高價值場景資料跨主體流通。(澎湃新聞)

### 2026-07-27 19:59:46  #市場 #國際

【高市內閣支援率大跌12個百分點】日本首相高市早苗內閣的支援率下跌勢頭不止，在最新民調中出現12個百分點的大幅滑落，不支援率則大漲13個百分點。7月以來日本媒體陸續釋出的民調結果中，高市內閣支援率持續下跌。時事通訊社民調結果顯示，高市內閣支援率較6月下跌5.3個百分點至49%；全日本新聞網的調查中，其支援率大跌10.9個百分點，至49.2%；日本《每日新聞》民調中，高市內閣支援率下降10個百分點至41%，不支援率則升至44%，首次超過支援率。即使在保守派媒體的民調中，高市內閣支援率也在“跳水”。《讀賣新聞》26日釋出的民調結果顯示，高市內閣支援率比上月猛跌12個百分點至57%，降幅為近期各主要媒體民調中最大。不支援率則大漲13個百分點至34%。高達71%的被調查者就高市內閣對高物價的應對給予負面評價，比上一次調查增加15個百分點。分析人士認為，在物價問題上未能回應民眾關切、消極回應政治醜聞削弱公眾信任、強推爭議性法案引發輿論反彈等，是造成高市內閣支援率大幅下滑的因素。27日，在日本國會眾議院預算委員會答辯時，高市被問及如何看待內閣支援率下跌，她以“我自己難以分析”“我不清楚原因”作答。（央視新聞）

US Futures Rebound on Monday — US futures rose on Monday, erasing the losses from last week as drop in energy prices softened worries of a deteriorating macroeconomic backdrop. Contracts for the S&P 500 and the Dow were around 1% higher, while those for the Nasdaq 100 gained nearly 1.5%. The US paused its strikes against Iran over the weekend and benchmark fuel prices eased on hopes that energy exports from the Middle East could return. The consequent drop in yields loosened financial conditions and nearly erased expectations that the Federal Reserve could deliver a rate hike this week, pushing back eventual tightening to September. Amazon, Meta, Microsoft, and Apple were up premarket ahead of their earnings in the coming days. Nvidia underperformed with a 0.5% gain after a $500 billion AI memory chip supply deal from SK Hynix and guaranteeing $250 billion for OpenAI to lease computing from a data center project, reigniting concerns that circular deals in AI computing could trigger contagion should spending ease.

### 2026-07-27 19:41:13  #Trading Economics #Economy #El Salvador #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

El Salvador Trade Deficit Widens in June — El Salvador's trade deficit widened to $1,030.96 million on June 2026 from $856.96 million in the same month a year earlier. Imports rose 16.5% year-on-year to $1,628.1 million, driven by purchases from Central America (4.7%), mainly Costa Rica, and Panama (63.5%). Meanwhile, exports rose at a slower pace of 10.5% to $597.2 million, supported by shipments to the UK (38%), Ecuador (25.2%) and Mexico (24.2%). In the January–June 2026 period, the trade deficit widened to $5,919.1 million from $5,302.4 million in the corresponding period a year earlier.

### 2026-07-27 19:38:54  #Trading Economics #Economy #Pakistan #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Pakistan Central Bank Holds Policy Rate at 11.5% — The State Bank of Pakistan left its benchmark policy rate unchanged at 11.5% at its July 27th, 2026 meeting, marking a second consecutive pause as policymakers cited an improving macroeconomic outlook. Headline inflation eased to 11.1% in June from 11.7% in May, reflecting lower global energy prices and favorable electricity tariff adjustments. Economic growth slowed to 4.0% in the first quarter of 2026, weighed by the Middle East conflict and the resulting rise in global energy prices. Looking ahead, the central bank expects stronger commodity-producing sectors to support services activity, with FY2027 GDP growth projected at 3.5%-4.5%. Inflation is forecast to gradually ease toward the upper end of the 5%-7% target range by June 2027. However, the outlook remains subject to uncertainty regarding geopolitical tensions and weather conditions, including El Niño effects, and potential fiscal slippages.

Wheat Eases from Over 2-Year High — Wheat futures fell below $6.70 per bushel, extending losses from the more than two-year high of $7.06 reached on July 22, as a sharp decline in crude oil prices weighed on agricultural markets. The US and Iran paused military strikes over the weekend following two weeks of hostilities, raising hopes that renewed diplomatic efforts could ease tensions and allow shipping through the Strait of Hormuz to normalize. Despite the pullback, supply risks remain elevated. Heatwaves across Europe and continued attacks between Russia and Ukraine have intensified worries over crop production and exports. Russian strikes on grain infrastructure and ports prompted Allseeds to suspend operations in Ukraine's Odesa region. Still, discussions on mechanisms to protect Black Sea shipping, including coordinated vessel notifications and temporary ceasefire windows, have raised hopes that grain exports can continue with limited disruption.

Soybeans Fall from Over 2-Year High — Soybeans fell to around $12.13 per bushel, retreating from the more than two-year high of $12.50 reached on July 24, as a sharp decline in crude oil prices weighed on agricultural commodities. Oil tumbled after the US and Iran paused military strikes over the weekend following two weeks of hostilities, boosting hopes that diplomatic efforts could ease tensions and restore shipping through the Strait of Hormuz. Despite the pullback, concerns over global crop supplies persisted. Heatwaves in Europe and continued attacks between Russia and Ukraine have disrupted agricultural trade, including damage to grain infrastructure and shipping routes. Meanwhile, Geneva-based vegetable oil producer Allseeds suspended operations in Ukraine's Odesa region due to intensified attacks on port facilities. However, discussions on measures to safeguard Black Sea shipping have raised hopes that export flows can continue with limited disruption.

### 2026-07-27 18:47:35  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China #Commodities #Energy

【NEA to advance eastern Ordos CBM transmission network under national ‘one gas grid’ plan】The National Energy Administration issued a 2026–2030 action plan to raise coalbed methane (CBM) reserves and output in the eastern Ordos Basin and directed coordinated build-out of transmission infrastructure. It calls for advancing a CBM transmission network in the eastern Ordos per the national “one gas grid” plan, focusing on Daning–Jixian, Mizhi North, Shenfu and Daniu-di blocks and leveraging existing oil and gas field infrastructure to strengthen network interconnectivity. The plan supports near-field hookups to national trunk pipelines including the Shaanxi–Beijing line, the West–East Gas Pipeline and the Yuji line, promotes digital and smart station upgrades to boost operational efficiency and refined management, and encourages planning and construction of underground gas storage to stabilise output and enhance peak-shaving and supply security.

### 2026-07-27 18:31:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Cbi Distributive Trades #Importance 2

UK Retail Sales Fall The Least Since January — The CBI retail sales balance improved to -26 in July 2026 from -54 in June, well above market expectations of -45, signaling the smallest decline in UK retail sales in six months. Although sales remained lower than a year earlier, retailers said the pace of the downturn had eased, while sales for the time of year were judged to be below seasonal norms by the smallest margin since February. The CBI said the sector continues to face headwinds from weak consumer confidence and elevated cost pressures, leaving the outlook subdued. Looking ahead, retailers' expectations for August improved to -26 from -45, the strongest reading since March. The CBI urged Prime Minister Andy Burnham's government to support high-street retailers through lower property taxes, address rising labour costs, and preserve hiring flexibility as employment reforms are finalized. Meanwhile, wholesalers reported broadly unchanged sales volumes for the first time in more than two years.

### 2026-07-27 18:28:13  #焦點 #國際

【商務部新聞發言人就美宣稱擬對中國人工智慧企業開展調查並實施制裁答記者問】問：近日，美國一些高階官員公開表示將針對中國人工智慧企業所謂“蒸餾”美前沿模型開展調查，並可能以“竊取美國智慧財產權”等為由制裁中國企業。請問中方對此有何評論？答：我們注意到有關情況。中方一貫反對美方將科技經貿問題政治化、工具化，以各種莫須有的理由汙名化中國企業並實施制裁。美方罔顧有關中國企業人工智慧模型與美前沿模型釋出時間非常接近、中國部分模型能力已處於領先地位等事實，給中國企業扣上所謂依靠“蒸餾”“竊取美國智慧財產權”等帽子，還威脅進行打壓制裁。這些做法缺乏事實依據，沒有法理支撐，在實踐上搞雙重標準，屬於典型的人工智慧霸權主義行徑。創新不是任何一方的專利。中國人工智慧企業長期深耕基礎研究，堅持科技自立自強與開放合作並重，秉持共商、共建、共享理念，推動人工智慧技術快速發展，在前端程式碼能力等越來越多的領域取得喜人成就。據瞭解，很多美國人工智慧企業在研發和訓練中蒸餾了中國的模型。值得注意的是，美業界眾多企業公開反對美國威脅制裁中國人工智慧企業。近200家美國初創企業敦促美國政府不要切斷對中國開源模型的訪問，認為這將削弱美國企業競爭力。一些美國大型跨國企業表示，模型蒸餾是一種行業廣泛應用的技術，應允許美國企業使用中國開源人工智慧模型。中方敦促美方傾聽中美兩國業界客觀、理性聲音，糾正霸權思維，停止對中國企業的抹黑和制裁威脅。對於任何實質性損害中方利益的行為，中方將採取一切必要措施，堅定維護自身正當合法權益。希望美方切實落實兩國元首共識，同中方相向而行，加強人工智慧對話、溝通，共同推動人工智慧向上向善，透過推進並完善人工智慧治理造福兩國和世界。

South African Bond Yields Fall as Oil Prices Ease Inflation Concerns — South Africa's 10-year government bond yield fell to 8.7%, retreating from last week's three-month high above 9.0%, as easing tensions between the US and Iran pushed oil prices lower and improved market sentiment. Brent crude declined from two-month highs after Washington and Tehran paused hostilities following two weeks of strikes, raising hopes for a diplomatic resolution that could reduce geopolitical risks and allow shipping through the Strait of Hormuz to resume. Last week, government bond yields climbed sharply as a surge in energy prices fueled inflation concerns and the South African Reserve Bank unexpectedly left its benchmark interest rate unchanged, defying expectations of a 25-basis-point hike. Meanwhile, annual inflation accelerated to 5.0% in June, its highest level in two years, while core inflation rose to 4.1%, the strongest reading since September 2024. Both measures remain above the upper end of the SARB's 3% ±1 percentage point target range.

【商務部新聞發言人就美髮布“強迫勞動”301調查最終措施答記者問】商務部新聞發言人就美髮布“強迫勞動”301調查最終措施答記者問。問：美東時間7月23日，美國貿易代表辦公室依據“未禁止進口強迫勞動產品”301調查結果，釋出最終措施，對包括中國在內的60個經濟體加徵301關稅，對華加徵12.5%的301關稅。請問中方對此有何回應？答：我們注意到有關情況。中方一貫反對強迫勞動，建立了完善的勞動法律法規體系，堅決防範和打擊強迫勞動行為。反觀美方，不僅仍未批准加入《1930年強迫勞動公約》，還長期操弄“強迫勞動”議題，此次又以“強迫勞動”為由發起301調查並加徵單邊關稅，是典型的單邊主義、保護主義行為，中方對此堅決反對。我們也注意到，美方多次表示擬透過301關稅替代已被判決失效的《國際緊急經濟權力法》（IEEPA）項下關稅和122進口附加費。美方在中美經貿磋商中明確承諾，對中國商品的替代加徵關稅不會超過20%。目前，美方對中國商品的替代加徵關稅為12.5%，中方對美第一輪所謂芬太尼關稅和對等關稅的反制措施仍然有效。我們將繼續密切關注並全面評估美方後續舉措，並保留採取一切必要措施的權利。中方敦促美方糾正錯誤做法，徹底取消有關單邊關稅措施。中方願在相互尊重、平等互利的基礎上，繼續與美方對話協商，解決各自關切。希望美方同中方相向而行，共同維護好落實好經貿磋商共識，不斷壓縮問題清單，拉長合作清單，為中美建設性戰略穩定關係作出更多貢獻。

South African Rand Recovers as Falling Oil Prices Improve Risk Sentiment — The South African rand strengthened to 16.70 per US dollar, recovering from an over three-month low of 16.98 reached last week, as easing tensions between the US and Iran triggered a sharp drop in oil prices and improved global risk appetite. Brent crude fell from two-month highs after Washington and Tehran paused hostilities following two weeks of strikes, raising hopes for a diplomatic resolution that could de-escalate the conflict and allow shipping through the Strait of Hormuz to resume. Last week, the rand weakened 2% against the dollar after the South African Reserve Bank unexpectedly kept its benchmark interest rate unchanged, against market expectations for a 25-basis-point hike. Meanwhile, South Africa's annual inflation accelerated to 5.0% in June, the highest in two years, while core inflation climbed to 4.1%, its strongest level since September 2024. Both measures remain above the upper end of the SARB's 3% ±1 percentage point target range.

【CCDC cuts core fees, fully waives Panda bond issuance registration fee】China Central Depository & Clearing Co. (CCDC) announced fee reductions for multiple core services and a full waiver of issuance registration fees for international (Panda) bonds. The interest/payment settlement fee for outstanding interbank market bonds is discounted to 95% of the current rate, resulting in 0.475 bps of face value. Interest/payment settlement fees for Sci-Tech innovation bonds and Panda bonds are fully waived. Spot bond settlement fees for bond types and issuers not covered by existing concessions will be cut 10% to 135 yuan/transaction. Settlement fees for pledged repos, buyout repos and bond lending in the interbank market are reduced to 95%: pledged repo single-security 114 yuan/tx, pledged repo multi-security 190 yuan/tx; buyout repo 190 yuan/tx; bond lending (including cross-custodian and centralized lending) 190 yuan/tx. OTC bond transaction settlement fees are cut to 95%, set at 0.19 bps of face value with a per-transaction cap of 120 yuan. All measures take effect from Sept. 1, 2026 through Dec. 31, 2028.

【中央結算公司：決定調降部分主營業務收費】中央國債登記結算有限責任公司釋出關於調降部分主營業務收費公開徵求意見的通知，具體如下：一、全額免除國際債券（熊貓債）發行登記服務費。二、對銀行間債券市場存續債券的付息兌付服務費費率實施9.5折優惠，優惠後費率為萬分之0.475。全額免除科創債、國際債券（熊貓債）付息兌付服務費。三、對銀行間債券市場現有優惠措施未覆蓋的券種和主體的現券交易結算服務費實施9折優惠，優惠後現券業務為135元/筆。四、對銀行間債券市場質押式回購、買斷式回購、債券借貸交易結算服務費實施9.5折優惠，優惠後質押式回購單券種為114元/筆，多券種為190元/筆；買斷式回購為190元/筆；債券借貸（含跨託管機構債券借貸、集中債券借貸）為190元/筆。五、對債券櫃檯業務各交易品種結算服務費費率實施9.5折優惠，優惠後櫃檯現券、質押式回購、買斷式回購、債券借貸、債券遠期等各櫃檯交易品種費率為債券結算面額的萬分之0.19，並實行封頂機制，單筆收費不超過120元。以上優惠措施自2026年9月1日起至2028年12月31日止有效。

【15th Five-Year disease prevention and control plan to push high-burden, cost-effective, fiscally affordable, internationally agreed vaccines into national immunization program】The State Council has approved and seven ministries — the National Health Commission, National Disease Control Bureau, National Development and Reform Commission, Ministry of Finance, Ministry of Agriculture and Rural Affairs, CUSTOMS, and National Administration of Traditional Chinese Medicine — jointly issued the 15th Five-Year Disease Prevention and Control Plan. The plan calls for dynamic optimization of the national immunization program and to include vaccines that are high disease burden, cost-effective, fiscally affordable and enjoy international consensus. It directs promotion of vaccination for priority groups such as middle-aged and older adults and women, aiming progressively for full life‑cycle, whole‑population immunization. The plan also pushes secondary-and-above medical institutions to provide vaccination services and mandates broader rollout of electronic vaccination certificates and cross‑region vaccination data sharing to improve accessibility and convenience.

【China issues Disease Prevention and Control 15th Five-Year Plan, targets modern disease-control system by 2030】The State Council has approved and seven agencies jointly issued the Disease Prevention and Control 15th Five-Year Plan. The National Health Commission, National Disease Control Bureau, NDRC, Ministry of Finance, Ministry of Agriculture and Rural Affairs, CUSTOMS and National Administration of Traditional Chinese Medicine set a 2030 goal to establish a modern, China-specific disease-control system, strengthen institutional frameworks to prevent and defuse large-scale epidemic risk, and operate a multi-trigger, fast-response infectious disease surveillance and early-warning system. The plan calls for substantial upgrades to scientific assessment, precision prevention, emergency response, clinical treatment and medical-supplies保障 capacity, breakthroughs in public-health research and workforce development, fuller preparedness for emerging infectious-disease outbreaks, effective control of key infectious-disease risks and containment of major disease harms.

### 2026-07-27 17:52:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Bahrain #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Bahrain Inflation Holds Steady at 2.3% — Bahrain’s annual inflation rate stood at 2.3% in June 2026, unchanged from the previous month and remaining at its highest level since May 2024. Prices increased at a faster pace for food and non-alcoholic beverages (5.8% vs 5.7% in May) and transport (10.6% vs 10.4%), while costs for education remained steady at 2.8%. Additionally, deflation eased for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (-0.6% vs -1.3%), clothing and footwear (-12.6% vs -12.7%), and furnishings, household equipment, and routine household maintenance (-1.2% vs -1.3%). In contrast, inflation slowed for miscellaneous goods and services (3.3% vs 3.4%), while prices declined for restaurants and hotels (-0.3% vs 0.2%), communication (-0.3% vs -0.2%), and recreation and culture (-0.6% vs 0.3%). Prices remained unchanged for housing and utilities (-2.2%) and health (-0.2%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.9% in June from 0.4% in May.

【工信部檢查埃安、小鵬，與電池故障有關？內部人士：沒聽說有必然關聯 或是全行業督查】工信部裝備工業一司近日赴廣汽埃安、肇慶小鵬開展道路機動車輛生產企業產品安全保障能力和生產一致性監督檢查。工信部重點檢查上述企業的智慧網聯汽車安全保障能力、設計開發及生產能力、生產一致性保證能力等生產准入條件保持情況，現場隨機抽取樣車、動力電池等，轉送至有關機構進行國家標準符合性等檢驗檢測。對此，有傳言稱，此次檢查可能涉及中創新航177Ah磷酸鐵鋰電池的質量問題。此前，搭載該電池的埃安S系列車型被曝出存在電池鼓包、漏液及行駛中突然斷電“趴窩”等故障。值得注意的是，中創新航不僅是埃安的電池供應商，同時也為小鵬供貨，而這兩家企業正是此次檢查的物件。對此，一位不願透露姓名的廣汽埃安內部人士告訴記者：“沒聽說（此次檢查與電池事件）有必然關聯，好像說是對全行業的一次督查。”另一位不願透露姓名的小鵬汽車內部人士則告訴記者：“可能不只是來廣州，聽說基本上規模車企都要去。” (每經)

【2.1萬億逆回購“護航”跨月！紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）成交7.78億領跑同類 機構：短期穩健為主】7月27日，三大指數集體上漲，創指漲超3%，滬指漲1.15%。在此背景下，紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）漲0.43%，報1.167元，換手率2.24%，成交額7.78億元，居同類標的ETF首位。訊息面上，跨月資金面成為市場關注重點。中國人民銀行此前公告，為更好地匹配銀行體系短期流動性需求，將在7月29日至7月31日、8月3日開展隔夜逆回購操作，採用固定利率、數量招標方式。其中7月29日至31日每日開展6000億元，8月3日開展3000億元，合計規模達2.1萬億元。華泰證券展望後市指出，公募中報顯示AI 行情交易和倉位擁擠度均處於高位、再均衡需求上升，但以配置係數衡量尚未達到極端水平，且本輪產業週期級別更高、覆蓋面更廣、容量更大。操作上，我們建議控制倉位，等待“超級周”後選擇方向。科技端關注國產鏈/半導體裝置、PCB、光模組等，兼顧創新藥、資源品、出海鏈、證券等再均衡機會，紅利繼續持有。光大證券認為，綜合來看，在政策積極維穩與結構性壓力並存格局下，短期市場大機率維持區間震盪。配置方向上，短期以穩為主，中長期關注以硬科技為代表的三條景氣主線。市場震盪整固期間，可適當配置紅利類資產，如銀行、公用事業等。投資者可將紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）作為底倉配置，無股票賬戶的投資者也可透過其場外聯接基金進行配置（A類：007466；C類：007467；I類：022678；Y類：022951）。

### 2026-07-27 17:27:48  #Trading Economics #Economy #Bosnia and Herzegovina #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Bosnia and Herzegovina Inflation at 4-Month Low — The annual inflation rate in Bosnia and Herzegovina eased to 4.2% in June 2026 from 5.6% in the previous month, marking the lowest level since February. Food and non-alcoholic beverage prices fell (-0.5% vs 0.6% in May), while prices for clothing and footwear declined further (-7.2% vs -6.9%). At the same time, inflation eased across several categories, including alcoholic beverages and tobacco (3.4% vs 3.5%), housing and utilities (10.5% vs 11.5%), furnishings, household equipment and routine maintenance (1.1% vs 1.6%), transport (17.5% vs 23.7%), recreation and culture (3.0% vs 3.4%), education (1.8% vs 3.4%), restaurants and hotels (4.9% vs 5.6%), and other goods and services (1.8% vs 2.3%). Meanwhile, inflation held steady for communication at 3.3%, while health inflation edged up (5.7% vs 5.5%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices fell 1.1% in June, following a 0.9% drop in May, marking a second consecutive contraction after eight straight months of increases.

【國務院辦公廳印發《關於國務院行政複議案件處理程式的若干規定》】日前，國務院辦公廳印發《關於國務院行政複議案件處理程式的若干規定》（以下簡稱《若干規定》），自印發之日起施行。《若干規定》旨在全面貫徹落實新修訂的行政複議法及其實施條例，提升行政複議監督質效，促進依法行政，進一步規範國務院行政複議案件有關處理程式。全文共10條，主要內容如下。一是授權國務院法治部門依法處理向國務院提出的行政複議申請，以及向國務院反映下級行政複議機關無正當理由不予受理、駁回申請或者受理後超過行政複議期限不作答覆的行政複議監督申請。二是明確各省、自治區、直轄市人民政府和國務院部門要加大行政爭議先行化解、調解、和解工作力度，積極配合國務院法治部門組織開展調解工作，推動將行政爭議有效化解在行政複議程式中。

### 2026-07-27 17:04:37  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovenia #Business Confidence #Importance 1

Slovenia Business Confidence at 2022-Highs — Slovenia's seasonally adjusted manufacturing confidence indicator improved to -2 in July 2026 from -4 in June, marking its least pessimistic reading since August 2022. The improvement was broad-based, while uncertainty eased to its lowest level since October 2025 (30 vs. 33). Production expectations strengthened (13 vs. 7), while the assessment of current production edged up to 5 from 4. Finished goods inventories also increased slightly (3 vs. 2). Order books improved overall (-16 vs. -17) and for exports (-18 vs. -20), while selling price expectations moderated (8 vs. 13). Employment expectations edged up to 8 from 7, although accumulation of labor eased (5 vs. 8). Outside manufacturing, confidence improved in retail trade, was unchanged in construction, and weakened in services, with sentiment in all three sectors remaining above their long-term averages.

Hong Kong Exports Reach Record High — Exports from Hong Kong surged 53.4% year-on-year to an all-time high of $641.1 billion in June 2026, accelerating from a 40.8% increase in the previous month. It marked the strongest annual growth since March 1984, driven primarily by robust shipments of electrical machinery, apparatus and appliances, and electrical parts thereof (57.2% vs 56.1% in May), office machines and automatic data processing machines (93.2% vs 50.2%), telecommunications and sound recording and reproducing apparatus and equipment (69.9% vs 37.6%), miscellaneous manufactured articles (58.6% vs 29.6%), power generating machinery and equipment (23.5% vs 7.6%), and professional, scientific and controlling instruments and apparatus (18.1% vs 7.3%). Regionally, exports to Asia climbed 54.4%, led by Mainland China and Hong Kong (59.2%), Singapore (83.0%), and Taiwan (79.9%). Shipments to other major markets also posted strong gains, including the US (114.3%) and Mexico (94.2%).

### 2026-07-27 16:50:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hong Kong #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Hong Kong Trade Deficit Narrows in June — Hong Kong's trade deficit narrowed to $52 billion in June 2026 from $58.9 billion in the same month a year earlier. Exports surged 53.4% year-on-year to a four-month high of $641.1 billion, driven mainly by stronger shipments of electrical machinery, apparatus and appliances, and electrical parts thereof (57.2%), and office machines and automatic data processing machines (93.2%). Exports to Asia rose 54.4%, while shipments to other key markets, such as the US (114.3%) and Mexico (94.2%), also increased. Meanwhile, imports climbed 45.4% from a year earlier to a three-month high of $693 billion, supported by higher purchases of electrical machinery, apparatus and appliances, and electrical parts thereof (54.2%) and telecommunications and sound recording and reproducing apparatus and equipment (67.3%). Import growth was particularly strong from Mainland China and Hong Kong (41.0%) and South Korea (176.7%). During the January–June period, the total trade deficit reached $294.6 billion.

### 2026-07-27 16:50:47  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hong Kong #Imports YoY #Importance 1

Hong Kong Imports Growth Surges to 1992 High — Imports to Hong Kong rose 45.5% year-on-year to USD 693 billion in June 2026, following a 42% rise in the previous month. The latest figure marked the largest import growth since February 1992, driven largely by increased purchases in electrical machinery, apparatus, appliances, and electrical parts thereof (+54.2%), telecommunications and sound recording and reproducing apparatus and equipment (+67.3%), and office machines and automatic data processing machines (+53.6%). By source, imports recorded the strongest growth among major suppliers from Korea (+176.7%), Vietnam (+106.8%), India (+95.4%), Malaysia (+62.4%), and Mainland China (+41.0%). For the first half of 2026, imports grew 40.6% compared to the same period a year earlier, lifted by higher inflows of non-ferrous metals (+197.8%), telecommunications and sound recording and reproducing apparatus and equipment (+71.5%), and electrical machinery, apparatus, appliances, and electrical parts thereof (+47.0%).

【IEA: Despite record prices, copper supply could face 25% shortfall by 2035】The IEA warns that despite copper trading at record highs, global primary copper supply could face a 25% shortfall by 2035 as mines currently under construction and planned are insufficient to meet rapidly rising demand. The agency attributes the gap to rising development difficulty and costs, driven by an approximately 40% decline in average copper ore grades since 1991. The shortfall estimate is based on current projects and existing policy settings. The IEA highlighted copper’s strategic role for clean energy, transport, construction, data centers and defense.

Armenia Trade Deficit Largest Since 2003 — Armenia's trade deficit widened to USD 540.2 million in June 2026 from USD 273.4 million in the same month of the previous year. It marked the largest trade shortfall since records began in January 2023, as imports rose 8.6% year-on-year to a four-month high of USD 1,096.9 million, while exports slumped 20.5% from a year earlier to USD 556.7 million. For the first half of 2026, the country's trade deficit expanded to USD 2.71 billion as exports declined 6.7% from a year earlier to USD 3.43 billion, while imports increased 4.0% to USD 6.14 billion.

【外交部亞洲司負責人敦促日方加快日遺化武處置程序】2026年7月26日，外交部亞洲司司長劉勁松赴吉林省敦化市督導哈爾巴嶺處理日遺化武作業，敦促日方全方位加大投入，加快日遺化武處置程序。劉勁松實地檢視日遺化武挖掘回收和銷燬等作業情況，檢查安全管理工作。在會見日方作業現場負責人及主要代表時，劉勁松嚴肅指出，日遺化武是日本軍國主義在侵華戰爭期間犯下的嚴重罪行。日方應切實履行國際公約和雙邊協議義務，向中方提供有效日遺化武埋藏線索資訊，加大投入，全鏈條加快處置程序，早日徹底清除日遺化武毒害，還中國人民以淨土。日方表示高度重視中方關切，將安全、高效推進處理日遺化武作業。劉勁松一行還於25日赴黑龍江省齊齊哈爾市，調研市博物館侵華日軍罪證實物展和侵華日軍第516部隊原址。（新華社）

【Ceasefire briefly capped oil gains; Hormuz flare-up revives rally】Last month Washington and Tehran signed a 14-point memorandum including a ceasefire, which briefly halted European fuel-price rises. Eurostat shows June diesel fell 6.4% MoM and gasoline fell 4.2% MoM versus May. Renewed tensions and fresh supply disruptions around the Strait of Hormuz, with no resolution visible, have pushed prices higher; global crude topped $100/bbl last week, the strongest level since early June. YoY fuel-price moves were largest in Bulgaria, Lithuania and Romania, each up more than 23%. Hungary maintains a fuel-price cap; Spain and Poland cut VAT on gasoline and diesel to blunt increases—Brussels has pushed for excise-duty cuts instead. Spain’s large downstream refining network limits its dependence on imported finished fuels despite heavy crude import reliance.

【Hong Kong close: Hang Seng up 0.98%; PCB stocks lead gains】After four straight weekly advances, Hong Kong stocks extended gains as the Hang Seng Index closed up 0.98%, trading as high as 25,276 after reclaiming the 25,000 level and the 100-day moving average. Sector winners included film and entertainment, travel, consumer electronics, new-consumer and PCB-related stocks; airlines and autos also rebounded. Lagging sectors included road transport, semiconductors and oil & gas, while optical communications and coal pulled back. Notable movers: MiniMax (00100.HK) +17%, Xiaomi Group (01810.HK) +7.3%, Laopu Gold (06181.HK) +5.5%; PetroChina (00857.HK) -3.5%, CSPC PHARMA (01093.HK) and China Resources Beer (00291.HK) both down >2.5%.

### 2026-07-27 16:12:20  #國際

【富途老虎期權內幕交易案鎖定賬戶超310個】圍繞富途控股和老虎證券美股期權的內幕交易案繼續發酵，越來越多的涉嫌非法獲利賬戶正浮出水面。此前，美國期權做市商海納國際（Susquehanna International）和城堡證券（Citadel Securities）已在美國紐約南區聯邦法院起訴“John Does 1 Through 100”——這是美國訴訟中常用於指代起訴時真實身份尚未查明的被告，並不意味著已經確認存在100名具體被告，而是為後續透過證據開示查明身份預留的法律佔位表述。 法院已於6月29日簽發臨時限制令，2026年5月7日至5月21日期間，透過盈透證券、富途和老虎等券商，買入富途和老虎短期看跌期權的賬戶被凍結。該限制令將“短期”定義為購買時距離到期日不足30天。 與此同時，法院亦批准原告啟動證據調查程式，允許其直接向盈透、老虎和富途發出第三方傳票，調取相關賬戶持有人身份、聯絡方式、賬戶資料及交易記錄等資訊，以查明匿名被告的真實身份。 （財新網）

Armenia Economic Activity Growth Slows in June — Armenia’s economic activity rose 7.7% year-on-year in June 2026, slowing from an 11.7% increase in May, which marked the highest growth since December 2025. Growth moderated in industrial production (4.0% vs 9.5% in May) and construction output (25.4% vs 27.1%). In addition, retail sales edged down 0.1%, after showing no growth in the previous month. Meanwhile, the volume of services, excluding trade, continued to rise, climbing 19.7% following a 14.7% increase. On a monthly basis, economic activity increased 4.4%, easing from 9.9% growth in May. For the first half of the year, overall economic activity was 7.9% higher than in the corresponding period a year earlier.

German Business Sentiment Improves for Third Straight Month — Germany's Ifo Business Climate Index rose to 86.6 in July 2026, marking a third consecutive monthly increase and slightly surpassing market expectations of 86. The latest reading points to a gradual stabilization in the German economy, although the outlook remains fragile amid ongoing tensions in the Middle East. Business expectations for the coming months improved to 86.7 from 84.3 in June, while firms' assessment of current conditions edged down to 86.5 from 87.0, suggesting that the recovery has yet to fully materialize.

### 2026-07-27 16:08:02  #其他

【機器人被列為中國“新新三樣”之一，今年相關企業註冊量同比增長19.24%】據央視財經訊息，機器人、人工智慧、創新藥正崛起為中國外貿的“新新三樣”。其中，機器人產業正實現從“炫技”到“真幹活”的轉身，市場關注點也從“能不能動”轉向“在哪跑通”和“怎麼批次造”。此外，資料印證了這一趨勢：上半年，清潔機器人和智慧仿生機器人合計出口180.9億元；工業機器人出口62.9億元，增長18.6%，銷往全球141個國家和地區；手術機器人出口4.8億元，猛增3.3倍，出口市場從23個擴大到49個。亮眼出口資料背後，國內各類機器人企業正緊抓海外市場機遇，加速場景落地、規模化量產與全球化佈局。企查查資料顯示，截至7月27日，國內現存機器人相關企業119.94萬家。近十年機器人相關企業註冊量持續增長，其中2025年全年註冊量首次突破20萬家，飆升至27.77萬家，同比增長39%。截至目前，今年已序號產生器器人相關企業15.55萬家，較去年同期增長19.24%。存量方面，從經營時間來看，超半數機器人相關企業成立於近三年，其中成立1-3年的企業佔比最高，達到29.33%。從城市線級分佈上來看，機器人企業集聚效應顯著，資源高度向高線級城市集中，其中新一線城市相關企業佔比30.86%位居首位，一線城市佔比23.28%。

【US-Iran pause raises hopes for Hormuz shipping; European gas and oil tumble】US and Iran paused mutual strikes over the weekend, boosting expectations that Strait of Hormuz energy flows could resume. ICE data at 07:35 GMT showed the Netherlands TTF near-month contract fell EUR4.69 to EUR58.89/MWh and the UK near-month contract dropped 11.44 pence to 142.67 p/therm. Prices had spiked last week to the highest intraday levels since March after 13 days of reciprocal attacks but eased to six-day lows following the pause. Oil prices also fell sharply. ING analysts said the move reflects a market eager for positive news, adding this is the first substantive sign of de‑escalation but the reasons remain unclear and vessel traffic through the Strait of Hormuz has not yet shown a meaningful rebound.

### 2026-07-27 16:04:43  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

UK Gilt Yield Falls Below 5% as Oil Prices Ease Inflation Fears — The UK 10-year gilt yield fell below 5% as declining oil prices helped ease short-term inflation concerns. Brent crude retreated from two-month highs after the U.S. and Iran paused hostilities and resumed diplomatic contacts, prompting money markets to slightly scale back expectations for further monetary tightening. Investors now turn their attention to the Bank of England's policy decision later this week, with policymakers widely expected to leave interest rates unchanged at 3.75%. Recent inflation data has reinforced that view, with annual consumer price growth slowing to a 15-month low of 2.6% in June, below the BoE's forecasts. A delayed pass-through of higher wholesale energy costs to regulated household bills has also kept UK inflation below that of the U.S. and the euro area, where the European Central Bank is still expected to raise rates again in September or October.

【河南省許昌市人大常委會原副主任劉林波被“雙開”】中央紀委國家監委網站7月27日通報，據河南省紀委監委訊息：經中共河南省委批准，河南省紀委監委對許昌市人大常委會原副主任劉林波嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，劉林波喪失理想信念，背棄初心使命，與他人串供，轉移隱匿證據，對抗組織審查；違反中央八項規定精神，收受可能影響公正執行公務的禮品、禮金、消費卡；違背組織原則，利用職權為他人謀取人事利益；廉潔底線失守，借用管理和服務物件車輛，將應由個人支付的費用由他人支付；大搞權錢交易，利用職務便利為他人在工程承攬等方面謀取利益，非法收受鉅額財物。劉林波嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律、廉潔紀律和生活紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大、十九大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經河南省紀委常委會會議研究並報中共河南省委批准，決定給予劉林波開除黨籍處分；由河南省監委給予其開除公職處分；收繳其違紀違法所得；將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

### 2026-07-27 15:53:14  #Trading Economics #Markets #Germany #Stock Market #Importance 1

DAX Extends Winning Streak on Improved Risk Appetite — Frankfurt's DAX 40 rose more than 1% to 25,440 on Monday, extending last week's 1.1% gain, as easing US-Iran tensions and lower oil and bond yields lifted risk sentiment ahead of a busy week of central bank meetings and earnings from major technology companies. Brent crude retreated from two-month highs, easing inflation concerns and prompting investors to scale back expectations for further policy tightening. Travel stocks led the gains on hopes that lower fuel costs will support margins and consumer spending, with Lufthansa jumping more than 3%. Chipmaker Infineon added 0.9% after the strong stock market debut of Chinese semiconductor firm CXMT, while construction group Hochtief fell 2.3% ahead of its quarterly earnings release after the close. Investors now await policy decisions from the US Federal Reserve and the Bank of England later this week, along with a fresh batch of European inflation data, for further clues on the interest rate outlook.

French Stocks Gain on Middle East Relief — The CAC 40 rose 0.7% to 8,428 on Monday, extending last week's gains as signs of de-escalation in the Middle East boosted market sentiment. The US and Iran halted hostilities following nearly two weeks of retaliatory attacks that had effectively derailed the June ceasefire. At the same time, Iranian and Omani officials discussed efforts to ensure the safe passage of vessels through the Strait of Hormuz, easing concerns over potential supply disruptions. Luxury stocks spearheaded the advance, with LVMH gaining 1.2% ahead of its first-half results release later today, while Hermès and Kering rose 2.3% and 2.4%, respectively. Other notable gainers included Airbus (+1.5%), Safran (+2.9%), BNP Paribas (+1.4%), and EssilorLuxottica (+2.1%). On the downside, TotalEnergies was among the few laggards, falling 2.4% as crude oil prices tumbled amid easing fears of supply disruptions in the Middle East.

【Strategists: Takaichi approval slide could weigh on yen and Japan bonds】Market strategists warn a decline in Prime Minister Takaichi’s approval could push the government toward looser fiscal and tax policies, increasing downside risk for the yen and Japanese government bonds. Japanese polls show her support has fallen to a post-inauguration low, though it remains above 50%. Nomura’s chief strategist said further slippage in polls could prompt the government to accelerate reflationary measures, negative for JGBs and the yen and potentially bearish for Japanese equities as a signal of weakened policy capacity. Takaichi’s campaign pledge to cut the food consumption tax has not been implemented and faces fiscal criticism. SMBC Nikko’s strategist added that public dissatisfaction over rising prices could spur further fiscal expansion and stronger measures.

FTSE MIB Open Higher — The FTSE MIB rose to around 52,000 on Monday, extending gains from the previous session, as risk sentiment improved following reports that the US and Iran had paused hostilities. The US extended a pause in airstrikes on Iran for a second night, while Tehran refrained from retaliation, sparking hopes of a broader de-escalation. The pause coincided with Oman-mediated talks on managing shipping through the Strait of Hormuz, potentially reducing risks to oil flows. The developments weighed on oil prices, with Brent crude sliding below $91 per barrel. On the bourse, heavyweight financial stocks led gains, including UniCredit (+1.7%), Banca Monte dei Paschi di Siena (+0.9%), Banco BPM (+0.8%), Assicurazioni Generali (+0.9%), and Mediobanca (+1.1%). On the flip side, oil major Eni fell more than 4%.

【Trillion-parameter model deployed; Meituan launches full-scenario AI Agent】LongCat2.0 was officially open-sourced this month. The 1.6T-parameter model has an average active size of about 48B (dynamic activation range 33B–56B) and natively supports a 1M-token context. It is the first trillion-parameter model to complete end-to-end training and inference on 50,000 domestically made compute cards. Meituan today launched CatPaw, a full-scenario AI Agent platform powered by LongCat2.0, aiming to move the model from "can run" to "use well." CatPaw provides an out-of-the-box AI workstation and hosted enterprise Agent development. The platform is already deployed internally at scale, covering 90k employees and 30k Agents, and has been validated in multiple live business scenarios.

The group plans to prioritise in-house exploration and reserve additions, complete a full resource inventory and speed conversion of resource potential, using subsidiaries and regional platforms to build a zonal, multi-point domestic resource-security network and strategic bases. 【外交部：申遺成功不是遺產保護的終點 而是新起點】7月27日，外交部發言人林劍主持例行記者會。有記者問，聯合國教科文組織第48屆世界遺產委員會會議審議透過了中國申報的“景德鎮手工瓷業遺存”專案，將其列入《世界遺產名錄》。請問中方對此有何評論？林劍介紹，7月25日，在韓國釜山舉行的聯合國教科文組織第48屆世界遺產委員會會議協商一致透過決議，將我國申報的“景德鎮手工瓷業遺存”專案列入《世界遺產名錄》，成為我國第61項世界遺產。聯合國教科文組織世界遺產委員會對該專案的真實性和完整性予以積極評價。林劍表示，申遺成功不是遺產保護的終點，而是進一步做好文化遺產傳承發展的新起點。中國願同國際社會一道，深入踐行全球文明倡議，認真做好《保護世界文化和自然遺產公約》履約工作。（央視新聞）

Euro Rebounds as Easing Middle East Tensions Boost Risk Appetite — The euro climbed back above $1.14, rebounding from near one-year lows reached last week, as easing tensions between the US and Iran and a sharp decline in oil prices improved risk sentiment. Brent crude retreated from two-month highs, helping ease inflation concerns and prompting money markets to slightly scale back expectations for further European Central Bank tightening, though they still price in nearly two 25-basis-point rate hikes by February 2027. ECB Chief Economist Philip Lane said the current inflation shock remains moderate, warranting some additional policy tightening but not an aggressive response. He also reiterated that inflation is expected to return to the ECB's 2% target over the coming year. Last week, the ECB left interest rates unchanged, as expected, while signaling that another rate hike in September remains possible. Investors now await fresh inflation data later this week for further clues on the policy outlook.

### 2026-07-27 15:30:53  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 2

Bund Yields Fall as Easing Oil Prices Temper Inflation Concerns — Germany's 10-year Bund yield fell to 3.14%, retreating from the 15-year high above 3.2% reached last week, as declining oil prices eased short-term inflation concerns. Brent crude retreated sharply from two-month highs after the US and Iran halted hostilities, fueling hopes for a diplomatic resolution that could restore shipping through the Strait of Hormuz. Money markets slightly pared expectations for further European Central Bank tightening but still price in nearly two 25bp rate hikes by February 2027. ECB Chief Economist Philip Lane said the current inflation shock remains moderate, requiring some policy tightening but not an aggressive response, while inflation is expected to return to the 2% target over the next year. Last week, the ECB left interest rates unchanged, following June's rate increase, while signaling that another hike in September remains a possibility. Investors now await a fresh batch of inflation data later this week for further clues on the policy outlook.
